==============================================================================================================
DECISION BOUNDARY
==============================================================================================================
This inspection does not declare the Universal Coordination Framework complete.

It determines which current production authorities can be reused for:
- job creation and submission;
- queue admission, claim and leasing;
- dependency readiness;
- state transitions and finalization;
- worker execution and dispatch;
- pipeline planning and manifests;
- persistence, snapshots and evidence.

Part C 7-B must freeze the canonical universal coordination contract
from this inventory before production implementation begins.

==============================================================================================================
FINAL RESULT
==============================================================================================================
PART C 7-A NOT_COMPLETE
